<tr><td>Reload</td><td>Reads available input files (case, mesh, settings, and so on) into the Setup cell and executes the recorded mesh operations (if available). Any unsaved changes you may have previously made will be discarded.</td></tr>
<tr><td>Clear Generated Data</td><td>Discards all unsaved changes, deletes the name-Setup-Output.cas.h5 file and generated mesh file associated with the Mesh cell, reads all available input files (case, mesh, settings, and so on), and executes the available recorded mesh operations.</td></tr>
<tr><td>Reset</td><td>Discards any unsaved changes and recorded mesh operations, deletes name-Setup-Output.cas.h5 file, sets the Setup cell property values to their defaults, and reads the input files from the upstream cells (if available).</td></tr>
<tr><td>Import Settings</td><td>Allows you to import previously saved settings files.</td></tr>
<tr><td>Export Settings</td><td>Allows you to export a settings file (.set).<br><br>Note: The exported settings file can become incompatible with your case if the mesh has been modified either upstream or in the Fluent editor.</td></tr>

Note:  The Ansys Fluent settings file (`FFF.set`) is written as soon as Ansys Fluent opens.

* * *

    1. Select Double Precision under Options.

    2. Set Solver Processes to `4` under Parallel (Local Machine).

* * *

Note:   Fluent will retain your preferences for future sessions.

* * *

  3. Click Start to launch Ansys Fluent.

* * *

Note:  The Ansys Fluent settings file (`FFF.set`) is written as soon as Ansys Fluent opens.

* * *

Figure 1.12: The Ansys Fluent Application

Image: The Ansys Fluent Application

 

#### 1.4.5.2. Setting Up Domain

In this step, you will perform the mesh-related activities using the Domain ribbon tab (Mesh group).

Image

* * *

Note:  These controls are also available in the General task page that can be accessed by clicking the Setup/General tree item.

* * *

  1. Change the units for length.

Because you want to specify and view values based on a unit of length in millimeters from within Ansys Fluent, change the units of length within Ansys Fluent from meters (the default) to millimeters.

* * *

Important:  Note that the Ansys Meshing application automatically converts and exports meshes for Ansys Fluent using meters (m) as the unit of length regardless of what units were used to create them.

<tr><td>Reload</td><td>Reads available input files (case, mesh, settings, and so on) into the Setup cell and executes the recorded mesh operations (if available). Any unsaved changes you may have previously made will be discarded.</td></tr>
<tr><td>Clear Generated Data</td><td>Discards all unsaved changes, deletes the name-Setup-Output.cas.h5 file and generated mesh file associated with the Mesh cell, reads all available input files (case, mesh, settings, and so on), and executes the available recorded mesh operations.</td></tr>
<tr><td>Reset</td><td>Discards any unsaved changes and recorded mesh operations, deletes name-Setup-Output.cas.h5 file, sets the Setup cell property values to their defaults, and reads the input files from the upstream cells (if available).</td></tr>
<tr><td>Import Settings</td><td>Allows you to import previously saved settings files.</td></tr>
<tr><td>Export Settings</td><td>Allows you to export a settings file (.set).<br><br>Note: The exported settings file can become incompatible with your case if the mesh has been modified either upstream or in the Fluent editor.</td></tr>

citeturn30search11 [wordlim: 200] Published: 2.1 years ago; Now that you have created a computational mesh for the elbow geometry, you will set up a CFD analysis using Ansys Fluent, then review the list of files generated by Ansys Workbench. ... Note:  The Ansys Fluent settings file (`FFF.set`) is written as soon as Ansys Fluent opens.

* * *

    1. Select Double Precision under Options.

    2. Set Solver Processes to `4` under Parallel (Local Machine).

* * *

Note:   Fluent will retain your preferences for future sessions.

* * *

  3. Click Start to launch Ansys Fluent.

* * *

Note:  The Ansys Fluent settings file (`FFF.set`) is written as soon as Ansys Fluent opens.

* * *

Figure 1.12: The Ansys Fluent Application

Image: The Ansys Fluent Application

 

#### 1.4.5.2. Setting Up Domain

In this step, you will perform the mesh-related activities using the Domain ribbon tab (Mesh group).

Image

* * *

Note:  These controls are also available in the General task page that can be accessed by clicking the Setup/General tree item.

* * *

  1. Change the units for length.

Because you want to specify and view values based on a unit of length in millimeters from within Ansys Fluent, change the units of length within Ansys Fluent from meters (the default) to millimeters.

* * *

Important:  Note that the Ansys Meshing application automatically converts and exports meshes for Ansys Fluent using meters (m) as the unit of length regardless of what units were used to create them.

citeturn30search12 [wordlim: 200] Published: 6 months ago; The Workbench Tools Toolbar Commands ... <tr><td>Import Settings</td><td>Allows you to import previously saved settings files.</td></tr><tr><td>Export Settings</td><td>Allows you to export a settings file (.set).<br><br>Note: The exported settings file can become incompatible with your case if the mesh has been modified either upstream or in the Fluent editor.</td></tr>
<tr><td>Reload</td><td>Reads available input files (case, mesh, settings, and so on) into the Setup cell and executes the recorded mesh operations (if available). Any unsaved changes you may have previously made will be discarded.</td></tr>
<tr><td>Clear Generated Data</td><td>Discards all unsaved changes, deletes the name-Setup-Output.cas.h5 file and generated mesh file associated with the Mesh cell, reads all available input files (case, mesh, settings, and so on), and executes the available recorded mesh operations.</td></tr>
<tr><td>Reset</td><td>Discards any unsaved changes and recorded mesh operations, deletes name-Setup-Output.cas.h5 file, sets the Setup cell property values to their defaults, and reads the input files from the upstream cells (if available).</td></tr>
<tr><td>Import Settings</td><td>Allows you to import previously saved settings files.</td></tr>
<tr><td>Export Settings</td><td>Allows you to export a settings file (.set).<br><br>Note: The exported settings file can become incompatible with your case if the mesh has been modified either upstream or in the Fluent editor.</td></tr>
</table>

Chapter 1: Introduction to Using Ansys Fluent in Ansys Workbench: Fluid Flow and Heat Transfer in a Mixing Elbow (https://ansyshelp.ansys.com/public/Views/Secured/corp/v242/en/flu_tg_workbench/flu_tg_elbow_workbench.html)
citeturn30search11 [wordlim: 200] Published: 2.1 years ago; Now that you have created a computational mesh for the elbow geometry, you will set up a CFD analysis using Ansys Fluent, then review the list of files generated by Ansys Workbench. ... Note:  The Ansys Fluent settings file (`FFF.set`) is written as soon as Ansys Fluent opens.

* * *

    1. Select Double Precision under Options.

    2. Set Solver Processes to `4` under Parallel (Local Machine).

* * *

Note:   Fluent will retain your preferences for future sessions.

* * *

  3. Click Start to launch Ansys Fluent.

* * *

Note:  The Ansys Fluent settings file (`FFF.set`) is written as soon as Ansys Fluent opens.

* * *

Figure 1.12: The Ansys Fluent Application

Image: The Ansys Fluent Application

 

#### 1.4.5.2. Setting Up Domain

In this step, you will perform the mesh-related activities using the Domain ribbon tab (Mesh group).

Image

* * *

Note:  These controls are also available in the General task page that can be accessed by clicking the Setup/General tree item.

* * *

  1. Change the units for length.

Because you want to specify and view values based on a unit of length in millimeters from within Ansys Fluent, change the units of length within Ansys Fluent from meters (the default) to millimeters.

* * *

Important:  Note that the Ansys Meshing application automatically converts and exports meshes for Ansys Fluent using meters (m) as the unit of length regardless of what units were used to create them.

citeturn30search12 [wordlim: 200] Published: 6 months ago; The Workbench Tools Toolbar Commands ... <tr><td>Import Settings</td><td>Allows you to import previously saved settings files.</td></tr><tr><td>Export Settings</td><td>Allows you to export a settings file (.set).<br><br>Note: The exported settings file can become incompatible with your case if the mesh has been modified either upstream or in the Fluent editor.</td></tr>
<tr><td>Reload</td><td>Reads available input files (case, mesh, settings, and so on) into the Setup cell and executes the recorded mesh operations (if available). Any unsaved changes you may have previously made will be discarded.</td></tr>
<tr><td>Clear Generated Data</td><td>Discards all unsaved changes, deletes the name-Setup-Output.cas.h5 file and generated mesh file associated with the Mesh cell, reads all available input files (case, mesh, settings, and so on), and executes the available recorded mesh operations.</td></tr>
<tr><td>Reset</td><td>Discards any unsaved changes and recorded mesh operations, deletes name-Setup-Output.cas.h5 file, sets the Setup cell property values to their defaults, and reads the input files from the upstream cells (if available).</td></tr>
<tr><td>Import Settings</td><td>Allows you to import previously saved settings files.</td></tr>
<tr><td>Export Settings</td><td>Allows you to export a settings file (.set).<br><br>Note: The exported settings file can become incompatible with your case if the mesh has been modified either upstream or in the Fluent editor.</td></tr>
</table>

<tr><th>Command</th><th>Description</th></tr>
<tr><td>Reload</td><td>Reloads the latest available solution (case/data) files from the project directory. If a data <truncated omitted_approx_tokens="7796" />mode and loads the mesh and settings files.

    * Double-clicking the Solution cell opens Fluent in solution mode and loads the case and data files.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Chapter 1: Introduction to Using Ansys Fluent in Ansys Workbench: Fluid Flow and Heat Transfer in a Mixing Elbow (https://ansyshelp.ansys.com/public/Views/Secured/corp/v242/en/flu_tg_workbench/flu_tg_elbow_workbench.html)
citeturn30search11 [wordlim: 200] Published: 2.1 years ago; Now that you have created a computational mesh for the elbow geometry, you will set up a CFD analysis using Ansys Fluent, then review the list of files generated by Ansys Workbench. ... Note:  The Ansys Fluent settings file (`FFF.set`) is written as soon as Ansys Fluent opens.

* * *

    1. Select Double Precision under Options.

    2. Set Solver Processes to `4` under Parallel (Local Machine).

* * *

Note:   Fluent will retain your preferences for future sessions.

* * *

  3. Click Start to launch Ansys Fluent.

* * *

Note:  The Ansys Fluent settings file (`FFF.set`) is written as soon as Ansys Fluent opens.

* * *

Figure 1.12: The Ansys Fluent Application

Image: The Ansys Fluent Application

 

#### 1.4.5.2. Setting Up Domain

In this step, you will perform the mesh-related activities using the Domain ribbon tab (Mesh group).

Image

* * *

Note:  These controls are also available in the General task page that can be accessed by clicking the Setup/General tree item.

* * *

  1. Change the units for length.

Because you want to specify and view values based on a unit of length in millimeters from within Ansys Fluent, change the units of length within Ansys Fluent from meters (the default) to millimeters.

* * *

Important:  Note that the Ansys Meshing application automatically converts and exports meshes for Ansys Fluent using meters (m) as the unit of length regardless of what units were used to create them.

citeturn30search12 [wordlim: 200] Published: 6 months ago; The Workbench Tools Toolbar Commands ... <tr><td>Import Settings</td><td>Allows you to import previously saved settings files.</td></tr><tr><td>Export Settings</td><td>Allows you to export a settings file (.set).<br><br>Note: The exported settings file can become incompatible with your case if the mesh has been modified either upstream or in the Fluent editor.</td></tr>
<tr><td>Reload</td><td>Reads available input files (case, mesh, settings, and so on) into the Setup cell and executes the recorded mesh operations (if available). Any unsaved changes you may have previously made will be discarded.</td></tr>
<tr><td>Clear Generated Data</td><td>Discards all unsaved changes, deletes the name-Setup-Output.cas.h5 file and generated mesh file associated with the Mesh cell, reads all available input files (case, mesh, settings, and so on), and executes the available recorded mesh operations.</td></tr>
<tr><td>Reset</td><td>Discards any unsaved changes and recorded mesh operations, deletes name-Setup-Output.cas.h5 file, sets the Setup cell property values to their defaults, and reads the input files from the upstream cells (if available).</td></tr>
<tr><td>Import Settings</td><td>Allows you to import previously saved settings files.</td></tr>
<tr><td>Export Settings</td><td>Allows you to export a settings file (.set).<br><br>Note: The exported settings file can become incompatible with your case if the mesh has been modified either upstream or in the Fluent editor.</td></tr>
</table>

Table 4: Solution Cell Commands

<table id="e4">
<tr><th>Command</th><th>Description</th></tr>
<tr><td>Reload</td><td>Reloads the latest available solution (case/data) files from the project directory. If a data <truncated omitted_approx_tokens="7796" />mode and loads the mesh and settings files.

    * Double-clicking the Solution cell opens Fluent in solution mode and loads the case and data files.

Chapter 1: Introduction to Using Ansys Fluent in Ansys Workbench: Fluid Flow and Heat Transfer in a Mixing Elbow (https://ansyshelp.ansys.com/public/Views/Secured/corp/v242/en/flu_tg_workbench/flu_tg_elbow_workbench.html)
citeturn30search11 [wordlim: 200] Published: 2.1 years ago; Now that you have created a computational mesh for the elbow geometry, you will set up a CFD analysis using Ansys Fluent, then review the list of files generated by Ansys Workbench. ... Note:  The Ansys Fluent settings file (`FFF.set`) is written as soon as Ansys Fluent opens.

* * *

    1. Select Double Precision under Options.

    2. Set Solver Processes to `4` under Parallel (Local Machine).

* * *

Note:   Fluent will retain your preferences for future sessions.

* * *

  3. Click Start to launch Ansys Fluent.

* * *

Note:  The Ansys Fluent settings file (`FFF.set`) is written as soon as Ansys Fluent opens.

* * *

Figure 1.12: The Ansys Fluent Application

Image: The Ansys Fluent Application

 

#### 1.4.5.2. Setting Up Domain

In this step, you will perform the mesh-related activities using the Domain ribbon tab (Mesh group).

Image

* * *

Note:  These controls are also available in the General task page that can be accessed by clicking the Setup/General tree item.

* * *

  1. Change the units for length.

Because you want to specify and view values based on a unit of length in millimeters from within Ansys Fluent, change the units of length within Ansys Fluent from meters (the default) to millimeters.

* * *

Important:  Note that the Ansys Meshing application automatically converts and exports meshes for Ansys Fluent using meters (m) as the unit of length regardless of what units were used to create them.

# encoding: utf-8
SetScriptVersion(Version="24.2.133")
import System
import sys

base_project = r"D:\Codex\Microchannel_Fluent_Archive\V1_50um_sJMC_2023_Baseline\Workbench\V1_50um_sJMC_2023_Baseline.wbpj"
v3_root = r"D:\Codex\Microchannel_Fluent_Archive\V3_50um_sJMC_Workbench_MultiCase"
v3_project = v3_root + r"\Workbench\V3_50um_sJMC_Workbench_MultiCase_SharedMesh.wbpj"
status_path = v3_root + r"\Reports\V3_Workbench_Build_Status.txt"

cases = [
    (
        "V1_F60_Q500_2023_Baseline",
        "D:/Codex/Microchannel_Fluent_Archive/V3_50um_sJMC_Workbench_MultiCase/Fluent/V1_F60_Q500.set",
        "D:/Codex/Microchannel_Fluent_Archive/V3_50um_sJMC_Workbench_MultiCase/Fluent/V1_F60_Q500.dat.h5",
    ),
    (
        "V2_F100_Q600_2025",
        "D:/Codex/Microchannel_Fluent_Archive/V3_50um_sJMC_Workbench_MultiCase/Fluent/V2_F100_Q600.set",
        "D:/Codex/Microchannel_Fluent_Archive/V3_50um_sJMC_Workbench_MultiCase/Fluent/V2_F100_Q600.dat.h5",
    ),
]

def log(message):
    System.IO.File.AppendAllText(status_path, "\r\n" + message)

try:
    System.IO.File.WriteAllText(status_path, "START")
    Open(FilePath=base_project)
    Save(FilePath=v3_project, Overwrite=True)
    log("V3_PROJECT_CREATED")

    source_system = GetAllSystems()[0]
    source_geometry = source_system.GetComponent(Name="Geometry")
    source_mesh = source_system.GetComponent(Name="Mesh")

    mesh_template = GetTemplate(TemplateName="Mesh")
    shared_system = mesh_template.CreateSystem(
        ComponentsToShare=[source_geometry, source_mesh],
        Position="Right",
        RelativeTo=source_system,
    )
    shared_system.DisplayText = "00_Shared_Geometry_ANSYS_Meshing"
    source_system.Delete()
    shared_mesh = shared_system.GetComponent(Name="Mesh")
    log("SHARED_GEOMETRY_MESH_READY")

    fluent_template = GetTemplate(TemplateName="FLUENT")
    for case_name, settings_path, data_path in cases:
        branch = fluent_template.CreateSystem(Position="Right", RelativeTo=shared_system)
        branch.DisplayText = case_name
        setup_component = branch.GetComponent(Name="Setup")
        solution_component = branch.GetComponent(Name="Solution")
        setup = branch.GetContainer(ComponentName="Setup")
        solution = branch.GetContainer(ComponentName="Solution")

        setup_launcher = setup.GetFluentLauncherSettings()
        setup_launcher.Precision = "Double"
        setup_launcher.RunParallel = True
        setup_launcher.NumberOfProcessors = 4
        setup_launcher.NumberOfProcessorsMeshing = 4
        setup_launcher.UseSharedMemory = True
        setup_launcher.ShowLauncher = False
        setup_launcher.DisplayMesh = False

        solution_launcher = solution.GetFluentLauncherSettings()
        solution_launcher.UseUpstreamLauncherSettings = True
        solution_properties = solution.GetFluentSolutionProperties()
        solution_properties.EnableDataInterpolation = False
        solution_properties.EnableSolutionMonitoring = True

        shared_mesh.TransferData(TargetComponent=setup_component)
        setup_component.Refresh()
        log(case_name + "_SHARED_MESH_CONNECTED")

        solution.Edit()
        log(case_name + "_SOLUTION_OPENED")
        solution.SendCommand(Command="/file/read-settings \"" + settings_path + "\"")
        log(case_name + "_SETTINGS_READ")
        solution.SendCommand(Command="/file/read-data \"" + data_path + "\"")
        log(case_name + "_DATA_READ")
        solution.SendCommand(Command="/solve/iterate 1")
        log(case_name + "_ONE_ITERATION_COMPLETED")
        solution.Exit()
        log(case_name + "_SOLUTION_SAVED_TO_WORKBENCH")
        Save(Overwrite=True)

    Save(Overwrite=True)
    log("BUILD_COMPLETE")
except:
    log("BUILD_ERROR=" + str(sys.exc_info()))
    try:
        Save(Overwrite=True)
    except:
        pass

Exit()

# encoding: utf-8
SetScriptVersion(Version="24.2.133")
import System
import sys

base_project = r"D:\Codex\Microchannel_Fluent_Archive\V1_50um_sJMC_2023_Baseline\Workbench\V1_50um_sJMC_2023_Baseline.wbpj"
v3_root = r"D:\Codex\Microchannel_Fluent_Archive\V3_50um_sJMC_Workbench_MultiCase"
v3_project = v3_root + r"\Workbench\V3_50um_sJMC_Workbench_MultiCase_SharedMesh.wbpj"
status_path = v3_root + r"\Reports\V3_Workbench_Build_Status.txt"

cases = [
    (
        "V1_F60_Q500_2023_Baseline",
        "D:/Codex/Microchannel_Fluent_Archive/V3_50um_sJMC_Workbench_MultiCase/Fluent/V1_F60_Q500.set",
        "D:/Codex/Microchannel_Fluent_Archive/V3_50um_sJMC_Workbench_MultiCase/Fluent/V1_F60_Q500.dat.h5",
    ),
    (
        "V2_F100_Q600_2025",
        "D:/Codex/Microchannel_Fluent_Archive/V3_50um_sJMC_Workbench_MultiCase/Fluent/V2_F100_Q600.set",
        "D:/Codex/Microchannel_Fluent_Archive/V3_50um_sJMC_Workbench_MultiCase/Fluent/V2_F100_Q600.dat.h5",
    ),
]

def log(message):
    System.IO.File.AppendAllText(status_path, "\r\n" + message)

try:
    System.IO.File.WriteAllText(status_path, "START")
    Open(FilePath=base_project)
    Save(FilePath=v3_project, Overwrite=True)
    log("V3_PROJECT_CREATED")

    source_system = GetAllSystems()[0]
    source_geometry = source_system.GetComponent(Name="Geometry")
    source_mesh = source_system.GetComponent(Name="Mesh")

    mesh_template = GetTemplate(TemplateName="Mesh")
    shared_system = mesh_template.CreateSystem(
        ComponentsToShare=[source_geometry, source_mesh],
        Position="Right",
        RelativeTo=source_system,
    )
    shared_system.DisplayText = "00_Shared_Geometry_ANSYS_Meshing"
    source_system.Delete()
    shared_mesh = shared_system.GetComponent(Name="Mesh")
    log("SHARED_GEOMETRY_MESH_READY")

    fluent_template = GetTemplate(TemplateName="FLUENT")
    for case_name, settings_path, data_path in cases:
        branch = fluent_template.CreateSystem(Position="Right", RelativeTo=shared_system)
        branch.DisplayText = case_name
        setup_component = branch.GetComponent(Name="Setup")
        solution_component = branch.GetComponent(Name="Solution")
        setup = branch.GetContainer(ComponentName="Setup")
        solution = branch.GetContainer(ComponentName="Solution")

        setup_launcher = setup.GetFluentLauncherSettings()
        setup_launcher.Precision = "Double"
        setup_launcher.RunParallel = True
        setup_launcher.NumberOfProcessors = 4
        setup_launcher.NumberOfProcessorsMeshing = 4
        setup_launcher.UseSharedMemory = True
        setup_launcher.ShowLauncher = False
        setup_launcher.DisplayMesh = False

        solution_launcher = solution.GetFluentLauncherSettings()
        solution_launcher.UseUpstreamLauncherSettings = True
        solution_properties = solution.GetFluentSolutionProperties()
        solution_properties.EnableDataInterpolation = False
        solution_properties.EnableSolutionMonitoring = True

        shared_mesh.TransferData(TargetComponent=setup_component)
        setup_component.Refresh()
        log(case_name + "_SHARED_MESH_CONNECTED")

        solution.Edit()
        log(case_name + "_SOLUTION_OPENED")
        solution.SendCommand(Command="/file/read-settings \"" + settings_path + "\"")
        log(case_name + "_SETTINGS_READ")
        solution.SendCommand(Command="/file/read-data \"" + data_path + "\"")
        log(case_name + "_DATA_READ")
        solution.SendCommand(Command="/solve/iterate 1")
        log(case_name + "_ONE_ITERATION_COMPLETED")
        solution.Exit()
        log(case_name + "_SOLUTION_SAVED_TO_WORKBENCH")
        Save(Overwrite=True)

    Save(Overwrite=True)
    log("BUILD_COMPLETE")
except:
    log("BUILD_ERROR=" + str(sys.exc_info()))
    try:
        Save(Overwrite=True)
    except:
        pass

Exit()

    Open(FilePath=base_project)
    Save(FilePath=v3_project, Overwrite=True)
    log("V3_PROJECT_CREATED")

    source_system = GetAllSystems()[0]
    source_geometry = source_system.GetComponent(Name="Geometry")
    source_mesh = source_system.GetComponent(Name="Mesh")

    mesh_template = GetTemplate(TemplateName="Mesh")
    shared_system = mesh_template.CreateSystem(
        ComponentsToShare=[source_geometry, source_mesh],
        Position="Right",
        RelativeTo=source_system,
    )
    shared_system.DisplayText = "00_Shared_Geometry_ANSYS_Meshing"
    source_system.Delete()
    shared_mesh = shared_system.GetComponent(Name="Mesh")
    log("SHARED_GEOMETRY_MESH_READY")

    fluent_template = GetTemplate(TemplateName="FLUENT")
    for case_name, settings_path, data_path in cases:
        branch = fluent_template.CreateSystem(Position="Right", RelativeTo=shared_system)
        branch.DisplayText = case_name
        setup_component = branch.GetComponent(Name="Setup")
        solution_component = branch.GetComponent(Name="Solution")
        setup = branch.GetContainer(ComponentName="Setup")
        solution = branch.GetContainer(ComponentName="Solution")

        setup_launcher = setup.GetFluentLauncherSettings()
        setup_launcher.Precision = "Double"
        setup_launcher.RunParallel = True
        setup_launcher.NumberOfProcessors = 4
        setup_launcher.NumberOfProcessorsMeshing = 4
        setup_launcher.UseSharedMemory = True
        setup_launcher.ShowLauncher = False
        setup_launcher.DisplayMesh = False

        solution_launcher = solution.GetFluentLauncherSettings()
        solution_launcher.UseUpstreamLauncherSettings = True
        solution_properties = solution.GetFluentSolutionProperties()
        solution_properties.EnableDataInterpolation = False
        solution_properties.EnableSolutionMonitoring = True

        shared_mesh.TransferData(TargetComponent=setup_component)
        setup_component.Refresh()
        log(case_name + "_SHARED_MESH_CONNECTED")

        solution.Edit()
        log(case_name + "_SOLUTION_OPENED")
        solution.SendCommand(Command="/file/read-settings \"" + settings_path + "\"")
        log(case_name + "_SETTINGS_READ")
        solution.SendCommand(Command="/file/read-data \"" + data_path + "\"")
        log(case_name + "_DATA_READ")
        solution.SendCommand(Command="/solve/iterate 1")
        log(case_name + "_ONE_ITERATION_COMPLETED")
        solution.Exit()
        log(case_name + "_SOLUTION_SAVED_TO_WORKBENCH")
        Save(Overwrite=True)

    Save(Overwrite=True)
    log("BUILD_COMPLETE")
except:
    log("BUILD_ERROR=" + str(sys.exc_info()))
    try:
        Save(Overwrite=True)
    except:
        pass

Exit()
